LATEST VISUAL PICKUP EXPERIMENT · NOT PROMOTED

Visual pickup remains unreliable.

1/5Trained RGB + proprio
2/5Blank-camera control
0/5Untrained control

The 201,672-parameter student trained for 257.3 s on 32 demonstrations. It receives two fixed RGB views plus joint and controller feedback. The trained candidate had 3 force-limit aborts in 5 trials.

Software information-boundary audits passed; a visual benefit is not demonstrated. The blank-camera control matched or exceeded the trained RGB outcome on this small paired set. No full visual assembly or hardware transfer is established, and final seeds remain untouched.

Inspect candidate, all outcomes and audits · Inspect blank-camera control · Earlier v1: 0/5 trained pickup

Student sensor contract and evidence

RGB[2,160,160,3] + measured q7/qd7/commanded targets7/previous actions7. No object pose, contact-force or task-phase input; no runtime teacher or IK. Hardware calibration, timing and gripper mapping remain unvalidated.

How far does the frozen skill transfer?

Same keyed peg and socket geometry. These wider pose and dynamics challenges test robustness without retraining; they do not establish generalization to new parts or hardware.

ChallengeMeasured successReset conditions
wider pose10/10Peg/socket XY ±10 mm; shared yaw ±0.15 rad; extra socket yaw ±0 rad
Mass/inertia 1–1×; friction 1–1×
dynamics10/10Peg/socket XY ±2 mm; shared yaw ±0.02 rad; extra socket yaw ±0 rad
Mass/inertia 0.7–1.3×; friction 0.65–1.35×
independent socket yaw10/10Peg/socket XY ±2 mm; shared yaw ±0.02 rad; extra socket yaw ±0.15 rad
Mass/inertia 1–1×; friction 1–1×
combined10/10Peg/socket XY ±10 mm; shared yaw ±0.15 rad; extra socket yaw ±0.15 rad
Mass/inertia 0.7–1.3×; friction 0.65–1.35×

CPU or GPU? The measured workload matters.

Actual micro-grasp training data on an Apple M4 Pro: two-thread CPU versus local Apple MPS GPU. Identical initialized weights and batch sequences; 400 total updates across two 1,540-parameter specialists.

Batch sizeCPU medianMPS medianResident-update result
5120.199 s0.355 sCPU 1.79× faster
40960.613 s0.456 sMPS 1.34× faster

Three repeats after warmup, with GPU synchronization. Allocation, transfers and optimizer setup are measured separately in the source report. Changing batch size measures throughput, not policy quality.

The original local loop took 19.74 s, including 4.99 s training. Rollout work includes both IK/controller computation and CPU physics/evaluation; moving only the network to a GPU would not move all of that work.

No A100 timing or end-to-end cloud speedup was measured. SmolVLA training is a different workload. Inspect raw timings and rollout profile.

Recorded results and decisions, newest first. No inferred live activity.

not evaluated0/5 measured successes

visual-student-v2-32episodes-retention-untrained

Visual learned action policy [learned] → Bounded joint delta and jaw command mapping [scripted]

Test whether physically scaled feedback normalization plus full32episode data and early-window sampling fixes initial closed-loop instability. This combined revision is not an isolated causal ablation.

Paired control0/5; comparison evidence only. Final seeds remain untouched.

Visual learned action policy [learned] → Bounded joint delta and jaw command mapping [scripted]

Test whether physically scaled feedback normalization plus full32episode data and early-window sampling fixes initial closed-loop instability. This combined revision is not an isolated causal ablation.

visual-student-v1

Visual learned action policy [learned] → Bounded joint delta and jaw command mapping [scripted]

A student trained on synchronized fixed-camera images and hardware-observable proprioception can imitate privileged demonstrations without object/contact oracle inputs at runtime; measure the first bounded candidate honestly.

Superseded before rollout by accurate8episode fixed-reset pickup experiments with explicit baselines/ablation.

skill-layout-cases-v1

Learned micro grasp [hybrid] → Learned linear alignment [hybrid] → Learned linear insertion [hybrid]

Test visibly different deterministic layouts with the same frozen skill chain; expose failure as well as success without fitting or discarding cases.

All6 predeclared named layouts succeeded, including±30° vertical key rotation. Deterministic examples, not a statistical success-rate or new-geometry claim; no fitting/promotion.
